\w[dark]\l[3]Would you like a basic overview of the Touhou Project series, or of Touhoumon Generation 1.8?
\w[dark]\l[3]Would you like a basic overview of the Touhou Project series, or of Touhoumon Generation 1.8?
\w[dark]Touhou Project is a doujin series produced by ZUN of Team Shanghai Alice.
\w[dark]Touhou Project is a doujin series produced by ZUN of Team Shanghai Alice.
\w[dark]\l[3]The official games are traditionally of the 2D-style shoot-em-up genre, with the occasional break for fighting games.
\w[dark]\l[3]The official games are traditionally of the 2D-style shoot-em-up genre, with the occasional break for fighting games.
\w[dark]\l[3]There is also an amalgamate of print works and Music CDs that are officially released by Team Shanghai Alice.
\w[dark]\l[3]There is also an amalgamate of print works and Music CDs that are officially released by Team Shanghai Alice.
\w[dark]\l[3]The setting is Gensokyo, a haunted region of Japan, that houses Humans, Youkai, Gods, and other supernatural and mythical entities.
\w[dark]\l[3]The setting is Gensokyo, a haunted region of Japan, that houses Humans, Youkai, Gods, and other supernatural and mythical entities.
\w[dark]\l[4]The plot of these games is self contained, usually not connecting to future or past games aside from one-off references, unless otherwise part of an arc.
\w[dark]\l[4]The plot of these games is self contained, usually not connecting to future or past games aside from one-off references, unless otherwise part of an arc.
\w[dark]As such, there is no definitive "order" to playing the games.
\w[dark]As such, there is no definitive "order" to playing the games.
\w[dark]\l[4]They usually revolve around the protagonists, Reimu Hakurei and Marisa Kirisame, traveling through 6 normal stages, attempting to resolve the current incident of the game.
\w[dark]\l[4]They usually revolve around the protagonists, Reimu Hakurei and Marisa Kirisame, traveling through 6 normal stages, attempting to resolve the current incident of the game.
\w[dark]\l[4]Incidents such as the Scarlet Mist incident, the Divine Spirits incident, the Urban Legend incident, and the Second Genso-Lunar War, just to name a few.
\w[dark]\l[4]Incidents such as the Scarlet Mist incident, the Divine Spirits incident, the Urban Legend incident, and the Second Genso-Lunar War, just to name a few.
\w[dark]\l[3]To learn more about the franchise, you can visit the wiki at https://en.touhouwiki.net/wiki/Touhou_Wiki
\w[dark]\l[3]To learn more about the franchise, you can visit the wiki at https://en.touhouwiki.net/wiki/Touhou_Wiki
\w[dark]\l[4]Touhoumon Generation 1.8 was a radical shift in the Touhoumon department. It was the introduction of several new concepts, and mechanics.
\w[dark]\l[4]Touhoumon Generation 1.8 was a radical shift in the Touhoumon department. It was the introduction of several new concepts, and mechanics.
\w[dark]\l[4]The most notable addition was the Diamond/Pearl Skill Split System, or as it's called in English communities, the Physical/Special Split.
\w[dark]\l[4]The most notable addition was the Diamond/Pearl Skill Split System, or as it's called in English communities, the Physical/Special Split.
\w[dark]\l[5]Alongside the Physical/Special Split came a completely overhauled type chart, featuring several new types that weren't in Pokemon and replacing some of the older types with the new ones.
\w[dark]\l[5]Alongside the Physical/Special Split came a completely overhauled type chart, featuring several new types that weren't in Pokemon and replacing some of the older types with the new ones.
\w[dark]The 1.8 Generation also had a more streamlined evolution system.
\w[dark]The 1.8 Generation also had a more streamlined evolution system.
\w[dark]\l[5]Unlike the previous Touhoumon generations, where Touhoumon would evolve in 3 Stages (Chibi-Normal-EX), with some special evolutions, this system had Touhoumon evolve from Chibi into several different forms.
\w[dark]\l[5]Unlike the previous Touhoumon generations, where Touhoumon would evolve in 3 Stages (Chibi-Normal-EX), with some special evolutions, this system had Touhoumon evolve from Chibi into several different forms.
\w[dark]\l[4]A vast majority of Puppets have Shard Evolutions, which evolve them into specialized forms, such as [A]ttack Forms, [D]efense Forms, and [S]peed Forms.
\w[dark]\l[4]A vast majority of Puppets have Shard Evolutions, which evolve them into specialized forms, such as [A]ttack Forms, [D]efense Forms, and [S]peed Forms.
\w[dark]\l[5]There are other, more non-specific, stone roles, such as [H]elper Forms, [T]echnical Forms, and [Ad]vent Forms. These roles cover a much different playstyle than the basic Attack, Defense, and Speed roles.
\w[dark]\l[5]There are other, more non-specific, stone roles, such as [H]elper Forms, [T]echnical Forms, and [Ad]vent Forms. These roles cover a much different playstyle than the basic Attack, Defense, and Speed roles.
